UEFA Team Coefficients 2007/2008
> 2009
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients 2006/2007
> 2008
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients 2005/2006
> 2007
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients 2004/2005
> 2006
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients (method=3) 2007/2008
> 2009
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients (method=3) 2006/2007
> 2008
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients (method=3) 2005/2006
> 2007
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients (method=3) 2004/2005
> 2006
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients 2003/2004
> 2005
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients (method=2) 2002/2003
> 2004
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients (method=2) 2001/2002
> 2003
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients (method=2) 2000/2001
> 2002
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients (method=2) 1999/2000
> 2001
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients 1998/1999
> 2000
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients (method=1) 1997/1998
> 1999
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients (method=1) 1996/1997
> 1998
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients (method=1) 1995/1996
> 1997
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients (method=1) 1994/1995
> 1996
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients 1991/1992
> 1993
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients 1988/1989
> 1990
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients 1985/1986

This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1983 <
UEFA Team Coefficients 1983/1984
> 1985
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients 1980/1981

This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients 1978/1979
> 1980
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients 1975/1976
> 1977
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1973 <
UEFA Team Coefficients 1973/1974
> 1975
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients 1970/1971
> 1972
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients 1968/1969
> 1970
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients 1967/1968

This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients 1965/1966

This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Team Coefficients 1963/1964

This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Since 1999 lines with country info (yellow) indicate the lowest possible value for any team of that country. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking. Clubs still in competition are indicated with a blue color, and clubs from active countries are indicated with a green color. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. This page shows the calculation of the UEFA team coefficients, which are determined by the number of points added to 20% (before 2009 33%, and before 2004 50%) of the country coefficient. Before 1999 these coefficients were given by the number of points divided by the number of matches. The coefficients of 5 succesive years are used for the UEFA team ranking.
